# Blockmancer Current Asset Usage Audit

## 1. Executive Summary
- Total referenced asset keys: **1145**
- Total physical asset files scanned: **615**
- Total confirmed used assets: **202**
- Total manifest/preload-only assets: **23**
- Total referenced missing assets: **870**
- Total orphan physical files: **413**
- Total placeholder/fallback assets: **50**
- Total legacy fallback references: **0** (plus active legacy alias codepaths)
- Top 5 risks:
  - High referenced-missing volume (870) against exact-frame animation contract; many assets are fallback-only at runtime.
  - Audio keys are referenced but physical audio files are largely absent (documented fallback-safe via AudioSystem tones).
  - Large orphan set (413) includes duplicate/legacy folders (for example `sprites/sprites/...`) that increases maintenance risk.
  - Legacy key compatibility remains active (`spr_*`, `ico_*`, `_frame_01` patterns) and can hide category/folder drift.
  - Stage/Boss arena assets for multiple stages are still reported missing in variant audit, impacting release presentation quality.

## 2. Methodology
- Scanned folders/files: `public/assets/`, `src/game/data/assets.ts`, `src/game/data/animations.ts`, `src/game/data/animation-standards.json`, `src/game/data/asset-display-rules.ts`, `src/game/systems/AssetSystem.ts`, `src/game/systems/AudioSystem.ts`, `src/game/scenes/`, `src/game/ui/`, `src/game/content/**/*.json`, `src/game/content/**/metadata.json`, `scripts/sync-assets.mjs`, `scripts/audit-asset-variants.mjs`.
- Reference patterns: `assetRefs`, `backgrounds`, `spriteKey`, `iconKey`, `portraitKey`, `backgroundKey`, `assetKey`, `imageKey`, `textureKey`, `vfxKey`, `effectKey`, `audioKey`, `sfxKey`, `bgmKey`, `musicKey`, direct `/assets/` references, and animation frame-key conventions.
- Status rules: `used_confirmed` = referenced key + physical file found at resolved/inferred path; `preloaded_or_manifest_only` = present in manifest but not content-referenced; `referenced_missing` = referenced key without matching physical file; `placeholder_or_fallback` = fallback keys/placeholder assets; `physical_file_only_orphan` = file exists in `public/assets` with no matched key reference dataset.
- Limitations: static analysis cannot fully prove dynamically generated keys at runtime; some resolver-inferred paths may differ from final runtime branch logic.

## 7. Hardcoded Asset Path Findings
| File | Line | Hardcoded reference | Recommended asset key approach |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| src/game/systems/AssetSystem.ts | 895 | `/assets/${category}/${key}.png` | Keep in AssetSystem resolver (acceptable), but avoid adding scene-level hardcoded paths. |
| src/game/systems/AssetSystem.ts | 905 | `/assets/icons/board-blocks/${key}.png` | Resolve through manifest-backed key first; keep as inferred runtime convention fallback only. |
| src/game/systems/AssetSystem.ts | 910 | `/assets/sprites/board-blocks/.../base/...` | Prefer explicit `assetRefs.base` when available; keep as fallback convention. |
| src/game/systems/AssetSystem.ts | 913 | `/assets/sprites/board-blocks/.../glow/...` | Prefer explicit `assetRefs.glow` and frame arrays; keep inferred path as non-breaking fallback. |
| src/game/systems/AssetSystem.ts | 916 | `/assets/sprites/board-blocks/.../clear/...` | Prefer explicit `assetRefs.clear` and frame arrays; keep inferred path as non-breaking fallback. |
| src/game/systems/AssetSystem.ts | 919 | `/assets/sprites/board-blocks/.../special/...` | Keep for inferred runtime convention only; new content should point to explicit `specialFrames` keys. |

## 8. Legacy / Fallback-Only Asset References
| Legacy key/path | Current preferred key/path | Referenced from | Safe to keep as fallback? | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| spr_block_* aliases | block_* | src/game/data/assets.ts, src/game/systems/AssetSystem.ts, src/game/systems/BoardSystem.ts | Yes | Compatibility mapping retained; do not remove until full content/asset migration complete. |
| ico_* fallback icon patterns | explicit iconKey/assetRefs.icon | src/game/systems/AssetSystem.ts + content JSON | Yes | Still heavily used by content; keep while filling final icon folders. |
| *_frame_01 old frame names | asset_id__animation_name__f00 | docs + metadata contract references | Yes | SOT marks this as fallback-only; avoid producing new `_frame_01` assets. |

## 9. Category Reorganization Recommendation
Recommended logical organization (no file moves performed):
- `public/assets/stages/{stage_id}/{battle|puzzle|map|boss-arena|route-scenes}` for stage/route backgrounds.
- `public/assets/sprites/heroes/{hero_id}/{state}/` and `public/assets/portraits/heroes/` for hero battle vs portrait separation.
- `public/assets/sprites/monsters/{monster_id}/{state|sheet}/` and `public/assets/sprites/bosses/{boss_id}/{state|sheet}/`.
- `public/assets/board-blocks/` for base stills; `public/assets/sprites/board-blocks/{block_id}/{base|glow|clear|special}/` for frames.
- `public/assets/icons/{items|spells|relics|upgrades|weapons|board-blocks|map|story-routes|status-effects|oopsies}` for icon taxonomy.
- `public/assets/effects/{vfx_id}/` and `public/assets/effects/items/{item_id}/...` for VFX sequences.
- `public/assets/ui/{buttons|panels|animations|story-routes}` for runtime UI skinning and animated UI states.
- `public/assets/audio/{bgm|sfx|ambience}` plus key-to-file mapping maintained in manifest.

## 10. Priority Fix List
- P0: Missing stage battle/parallax and boss arena assets for release stages; missing core monster/boss sheets and key hero animation states called out by `validate:animations` and `audit:asset-variants`.
- P1: Hero/monster/boss icon and portrait completeness (`ico_*`, `portrait_icon`, pose sheets) to stabilize release presentation.
- P2: Fill exact-frame VFX sequences and spell/item VFX gaps currently fallback-safe but presentation-incomplete.
- P3: Legacy cleanup (`spr_*`, duplicate `sprites/sprites/` paths, typo families like `block_tolbox`) after compatibility window.

## 11. Commands Run
- `npm run validate:content` -> failed under PowerShell policy (`npm.ps1` blocked); rerun succeeded with `npm.cmd run validate:content`: **passed** (`335 JSON files`, `36 route scenes`).
- `npm run validate:metadata` -> failed under PowerShell policy; rerun with `npm.cmd`: **passed**.
- `npm run validate:animations` -> failed under PowerShell policy; rerun with `npm.cmd`: **passed with warnings** (`384` definitions, `1629` missing expected frame files, `42` missing preferred monster/boss sheets).
- `npm run sync:assets` -> **failed** (`Cannot find module scripts/sync-asset-runtime-map.mjs`).
- `npm run audit:asset-variants` -> **passed with warnings** (`122` warnings).
- `npm run build` -> **passed** (TypeScript + Vite build succeeded).
- Additional static aid: `node scripts/sync-assets.mjs` -> completed (`Runtime asset-like keys: 448`, `Physical assets: 615`, `Missing primary: 448`, `Orphan physical: 50`).

## 12. Known Limitations
- Dynamic key generation and resolver fallbacks in `AssetSystem` can resolve assets not directly inferable from static key-path heuristics.
- Some inferred expected paths differ from current on-disk conventions (`icons/ico_*` vs `icons/<category>/...`, `special` vs named animation subfolders), which inflates static `referenced_missing` counts.
- This audit is intentionally non-destructive: no asset moves/renames/deletes and no save/runtime ID rewrites were performed.
